```json
      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"推理+评测完整流程: 加载 Qwen3-4B + LoRA adapter → 批量推理 → 解析输出 → 计算 F1/Evi-F1/EDCR。\\n\\n输入:\\n  - base model (Qwen3-4B) + 可选 LoRA adapter (SFT/GRPO checkpoint)\\n  - DocRED JSON 数据目录 (含 dev.json, train_annotated.json)\\n\\n输出 (保存到 --output_dir):\\n  - metrics.json: F1, Ign-F1, Evi-F1, EDCR, format_compliance_rate, n_truncated 等\\n  - predictions.json: 每个文档的详细预测 (doc_id, raw_output, parsed_triples, gold_triples, truncated, format_ok)\\n\\n依赖: transformers, peft, bitsandbytes, torch, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom peft import PeftModel\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, BitsAndBytesConfig\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nfrom freige.data.docred_processor import DocREDProcessor, SPLIT_FILENAMES\\nfrom freige.eval.evaluator import DocREDEvaluator, gold_from_docred, parse_model_output\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"You are an information extraction model. Given a document with numbered sentences \\\"\\n    \\\"and a list of entities, extract all relation triples. ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# A. 模型加载\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef load_model_and_tokenizer(model_path, adapter_path=None, sft_adapter_path=None, quantize=True):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"加载 Qwen3-4B + 可选 LoRA adapter (4-bit QLoRA)。\\n\\n    Args:\\n        model_path: base model 路径\\n        adapter_path: LoRA adapter 路径（SFT 或 GRPO checkpoint），None 则不加载\\n    Returns:\\n        (model, tokenizer)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading tokenizer: %s\\\", oracle ceiling 的 37% (0.0807/0.218)。CED-F1 Spearman r=0.3235 (p≈0)，相关性显著但中等——说明 CED 不是完美的 proxy 但有信号。Random baseline rel_f1=0.4538 说明 sampling diversity 本身就有 +4.8pp 收益，CED 在此基础上再加 +3.3pp。EDCR 下降 (0.6156 vs SFT 0.7118)，N=8 sampling 引入更多 distractor citations。推理耗时 5.8h (N=8 生成主导)。结论：CED 作为 inference-time reranker 有明确论文价值——零训练成本超越 RSFT，且 oracle ceiling 表明更好的 reward model 还有大幅空间。\"}, <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>
```

```text
Now I have the full picture. The worker `worker_ops_plan_001_flatnli_rsft_train_20260515T145109` was responsible for the flat-NLI RSFT training pipeline (plan_001). Looking at the timeline:

1. **plan_001_flat_nli_scoring** (completed 15:16 UTC May 15) — flat-NLI scoring step
2. **plan_001_rsft_flat_nli** (completed 18:04 UTC May 15) — RSFT training with flat-NLI scored data, seed=42
   - train_loss=0.6238, epochs=3, total_steps=507
   - checkpoint: /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_flat_nli/checkpoint-507
3. **eval_plan_001_flat_nli** (completed 18:04 UTC May 15) — Dev eval of the trained model
   - rel_f1=0.4539, ign_f1=0.4539, evi_f1=0.8043, edcr=0.6431

Then additional seeds were trained: seed 43 finished at 20:28 UTC and seed 45 at 23:10 UTC on May 15.

6. **plan_001_flat_nli_seed44** (completed 23:25 UTC May 15)

The corresponding evals came back with rel_f1 scores of 0.4523 for seed 43, 0.4615 for seed 44, and 0.4551 for seed 45, all completed by early morning on May 16. I'm now trying to figure out whether these additional seeds came from the same worker or different ones, and I need to verify the training specifications and checkpoint locations to understand the full pipeline.

Looking at the actual training and evaluation setup, I'm noticing some inconsistencies. The flat NLI training used 2691 samples over 3 epochs with 507 steps, but the evaluation was run with batch_size=16, which violates the D076 protocol that requires batch_size=4. More importantly, I'm seeing that ign_f1 equals rel_f1 at 0.4539, which shouldn't happen—these metrics should differ because ign_f1 excludes entity pairs that appear in the training set. This exact equality is actually the bug that prompted the whole ign_f1 fix effort in the first place.

The seed_comparison_prep notes confirm this: the original eval at seed 42 had this buggy ign_f1 computation where it matched rel_f1 exactly, and there's no metrics_v2.json with the corrected values. The reeval_flat_nli_s42 task is meant to address this by re-running evaluation with the proper batch_size=4 and the fixed ign_f1 calculation. So the worker that ran the initial training and dev eval was actually using non-compliant settings and buggy metrics all along.

Now I need to check what the actual training configuration looks like on disk to understand what parameters were really used.
```

```text
**What the worker did:**

2. flat-NLI RSFT training (plan_001_rsft_flat_nli) — completed 18:04 UTC May 15
   - Base model: Qwen3-4B
   - LoRA config: r=64, lora_alpha=128, lora_dropout=0.05
   - Applied to q/k/v/o/gate/down/up_proj layers across 2691 training samples for 3 epochs (507 steps total), achieving a train loss of 0.6238 with the checkpoint saved at /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_flat_nli/checkpoint-507
3. Dev eval (eval_plan_001_flat_nli) — completed 18:04 UTC May 15
   - Ran evaluation with batch_size=16 without quantization and using the SFT adapter, but the results show a bug where rel_f1 and ign_f1 are identical at 0.4539, and evi_f1 appears to be 0

.8043 with edcr=0.6431
   - The metrics.json file confirms this is a known issue: the ignored F1 computation failed to exclude training-set entity pairs, so ign_f1 is just a copy of rel_f1 rather than a distinct metric — this is what the reeval_flat_nli_s42 re-evaluation is meant to address, though this particular eval used batch_size=16 instead of the required D076 protocol with batch_size=4

2. **[INFO] Training config consistent**
   - LoRA setup matches CED RSFT: r=64, lora_alpha=128 with the same target modules and 3 epochs
   - Flat-NLI ran 507 steps on 2691 samples versus CED RSFT's 510 steps on 2718 samples — the slight difference in sample counts is expected given different filtering

3. **[INFO] Eval protocol non-D076**
   - This eval used batch_size=16, which doesn't meet D076 requirements, so it's being re-run as reeval_flat_nli_s42 with proper protocol

4. **[WARNING] model_path configuration issue**
   - The eval_plan_001_flat_nli spec points to the adapter root directory rather than the specific checkpoint-507 directory

, though the root does contain the final adapter weights copied from checkpoint-507, so functionally it works but differs from how the reeval explicitly specifies the checkpoint

5. **[INFO] Metrics registry ↔ disk consistent**
   - Registry and disk values match: rel_f1=0.4539, evi_f1=0.8043, edcr=0.6431

6. **GPU status**: All 4 GPUs are actively running reevals with no idle or suspicious states.

The flat-NLI RSFT training and eval pipeline completed successfully overall, though the main issue stems from the ign_f1 bug in the eval code itself—a pre-existing problem unrelated to this worker. Since the re-evaluation is already underway and this is an older worker from May 15 whose results have been superseded, I'll note the bug for the record but it's already being handled.
```

```json
  "text": "## 审查报告 — worker_ops_plan_001_flatnli_rsft_train (plan_001)\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n### Worker 完成内容\n完整的 flat-NLI RSFT pipeline：(1) flat-NLI scoring → (2) RSFT 训练 (seed=42, 2691 samples, 3 epochs, 507 steps) → (3) dev eval。\n\n训练配置与 CED RSFT 一致：LoRA r=64, alpha=128, target=q/k/v/o/gate/down/up_proj，base=Qwen3-4B。\n\n### 发现\n\n- [severity: warning] **Ign-F1 计算 bug 确认**：eval_plan_001_flat_nli 的 metrics.json 中 `ign_f1=0.4538761002031144` 与 `f1=0.4538761002031144` 完全相同，`ign_precision==precision`, `ign_recall==recall`。说明 Ign-F1 没有排除 train set 共享实体对，ign_* 指标是 non-ign 的副本。Registry 记录的 ign_f1=0.4539 不可引用于论文。**reeval_flat_nli_s42 正在用 D076 协议 + fixed Ign-F1 重跑，修复路径正确。**\n\n- [severity: warning] **eval 用了 batch_size=16（非 D076 统一协议）**：eval_plan_001_flat_nli 的 config.batch_size=16。D076 统一协议要求 batch_size=4。该 eval 结果（rel_f1=0.4539）不能直接与 D076 协议下的 CED RSFT seeds 对比。reeval_flat_nli_s42 已在修复。\n\n- [severity: info] **训练配置与 CED RSFT 一致**：flat-NLI 和 CED RSFT 使用相同 LoRA 配置（r=64, alpha=128）、相同 base model、相同 epoch 数。训练样本数略有差异（flat-NLI 2691 vs CED 2718）源于 scoring 筛选差异，合理。CED 507 vs CED 510 步也由样本数差异引起。对照组设计合理，只改了 scoring 方法这一个变量。\n\n- [severity: info] **指标 registry ↔ 磁盘一致**：rel_f1=0.4539, evi_f1=0.8043, edcr=0.6431 三方吻合。\n\n- [severity: info] **model_path 指向 adapter 根目录而非 checkpoint-507**：eval 命令 `--model_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_flat_nli`，根目录 adapter 与 checkpoint-507 相同（训练完成时 copy）。reeval 已改用显式 `checkpoint-507` 路径，更规范。\n\n### 结论\n训练本身无问题，实验设计合理（单变量对照）。两个 warning 均是已知问题且修复已在进行中（reeval_flat_nli_s42）。该 worker 的原始 eval 数据（rel_f1=0.4539, ign_f1=0.4539）**不应用于论文**，需等待 D076 reeval 结果替代。",

}
```

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

1. Primary Request and Intent:
   I am acting as the **independent scientific reviewer** for the "faith-evidence-ie" project. My role is to audit experiment conclusions, verify metrics consistency, check GPU resource compliance (L1 red lines), and report findings to the Director via `reviewer_post_to_director`. I respond only in Chinese. I received a series of `worker_done` and `cron:on_complete` notifications and was asked to review each worker's results. My primary ongoing task is verifying the seed comparison campaign (CED RSFT vs flat-NLI RSFT, 4 seeds each) under the D076 unified evaluation protocol (batch_size=4, --no-quantize, --sft_adapter, max_new_tokens=1024).

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **D076 Unified Protocol**: Standardized eval settings (bs=4, no-quantize, sft_adapter, max_new_tokens=1024) to enable fair paired t-test across seeds
   - **Ign-F1 bug fix**: Original evals had ign_f1 == rel_f1 (bug in train_facts filtering). Fixed via metrics_v2.json with "doc_id-free train_facts"
   - **RSFT (Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning)**: CED-scored vs flat-NLI-scored training data selection
   - **CED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination)**: NLI-based evidence scoring using contrastive margins
   - **EDCR (Evidence Distractor Citation Ratio)**: Novel metric measuring citation precision
   - **LoRA config**: r=64, lora_alpha=128, dropout=0.05, bf16, target_modules=[q,k,v,o,gate,up,down_proj]
   - **Qwen3-4B**: Base model for all experiments
   - **Constraint C001**: GPU memory must be < 2GB before submitting eval
   - **4 active constraints** (C001-C004) governing GPU usage and process management

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/freige/eval/inference.py`** (remote server)
     - Core eval pipeline used by all experiments

```text
   - GPU 1: 46%, 10207/32607 MiB — sft_baseline_d076
   - GPU 2: 45%, 11091/32607 MiB — ced_s42
   - GPU 3: 0%, 0 MiB — idle

3. **seed_ttest_results.json analysis** (critical review...

Looking at the paired t-test results from seeds s43 and s45, the data shows no statistically significant differences between CED and flat-NLI across any metrics—F1, Ign_F1, Evi_F1, and EDCR all have p-values above 0.6, with flat-NLI showing only marginal improvements on F1.

The sample size of n=2 is severely underpowered for statistical testing. I'm still waiting on results from CED s42, flat_NLI s42, and CED s44, plus the sft_baseline_d076_reeval evaluation which is still running. I should also verify the GPU allocation since both reeval_flat_nli_s42 and reeval_ced_s44 are currently occupying cuda:0. the GPU whitelist and review the t-test results critically. The n=2 paired t-test is a major concern — with only 1 degree of freedom, statistical power is essentially zero. The p-values are meaningless at this sample size.

Also need to verify: the per-seed data matches what I verified earlier:
- CED s43: ign_f1=0.451169 — I previously verified 0.4512 ✓
- CED s45: ign_f1=0.441555 — I previously verified 0.4416 ✓  
- flatNLI s43: ign_f1=0.450267 — I previously verified 0.4503 ✓
- flatNLI s45: ign_f1=0.446181 — I previously verified 0.4462 ✓

The per-seed data checks out. Once the missing seeds (s42 and s44) finish running, I'll have n=4 matched pairs, which gives df=3 and at least some statistical possibility, though the effect sizes are tiny — only about 0.19 percentage points difference in ignored F1 — well within noise margins.

But there's something more important I'm noticing: the SFT-noevi baseline actually has higher relation F1 than both RSFT variants, which means adding evidence generation is hurting relation extraction performance. That's a significant finding for how we frame the paper's results.

Let me verify the protocol for these comparisons — the t-test results show CED versus flat-NLI RSFT as the main paired comparison, with SFT-noevi serving as a reference baseline rather than part of the direct comparison. Now I should check the training logs to see how much longer the running experiments will take.
```
